             The Group’s finance department includes a team that                       本 集 團 之 財 務 部 設 有 一 個 小 組， 負 責
             performs the valuation of financial assets required for                   就 財 務 報 告 事 宜 執 行， 並 在 需 要 時 在

             independent valuer when necessary, including Level                        行 估 值， 包 括 有 關 第 三 層 公 允 價 值。
             3 fair values. This team reports directly to the chief                    此 小 組 直 接 向 財 務 總 監（「財 務 總 監 」）
             financial officer (“CFO”). Discussions of valuation                       匯 報。 財 務 總 監 及 評 估 小 組 乃 按 本 集
             processes and results are held between the CFO and the                    團之報告日期即每半年舉行一次討論
             valuation team at least once for every half a year, in line               估值流程與其結果。
             with the Group’s reporting dates.

      Segment result represents the profit before income tax                    分部業績是代表各分部之除稅前盈利，當中
      earned by each segment without inclusion of unallocated                   並沒有包括未分類的公司開支、融資成本、
      corporate expenses, finance costs, finance income, share of               財務收入，應佔聯營公司及共同控制實體之
      results of associates and jointly controlled entities. This is the        業績。此分部業績主要是為分配資源和評估
      measure reported to chief operating decision makers for the               各分部之表現而向主要營運決策者呈列的方
      purposes of resource allocation and assessment of segment                 式。
      performance.
